# Tavue dish photographs

These photographs are individually licensed, not owned by Tavue. App thumbnails crop to their display frame; full images are available in the photo viewer. Resized Commons thumbnails are bundled without further image edits. Each photograph and any adaptation retain the listed licence. This document and the in-app photo viewer preserve author, title, source and licence links.
